Instrumentation highlights

The science runner now stores per-model convergence diagnostics (iterations, gradient norm, Hessian diagonals, stop reason/tolerance) alongside runtime metrics (worker count, CPU time, RSS, GPU utilization via nvidia-smi, and batch/evaluation counts).

These details feed the dataset-quality grid above and the convergence/performance panels in each model section so analysts can assess optimizer behavior, resource cost, and data conditioning in one place.
